- **code-reviewer**: Independent senior-engineer review of code changes before merge. Finds bugs, regressions, missed edge cases, incorrect error handling, data-integrity, security, and concurrency risks, resource leaks, meaningful performance problems, and maintainability issues that raise defect risk. Delegate to it to review a diff, staged or unstaged changes, a commit, a branch or PR, or specific files, or to get a second opinion on code just written. Read-only. Returns a severity-ranked findings report with file:line references, not a patch.
